PATCH
Auction House BIN + Cancels
Updated: May 19, 2020
Hello.
Two new [[Auction House]] features!
### BIN Auctions
You can now create Auctions at a fixed price which instantly sells to the item buyer.
Create a BIN using this button when you create an Auction
You can buy BIN items from anywhere, but it will only auto-claim when in the hub:
### Auctions Cancellation
You may now cancel your [[Auction House|Auctions]] if they have 0 bids / haven't been purchased.
### Other
Changed the rebidding minimum from 15% to:
- Less than 100k: 15%
- 100k to 1m: 10%
- 1m to 10m: 5%
- 10m and more: 2.5%
Enchanted books now have a rarity
- Players can't [[Auction House|Auction]] [[Bazaar]] products anymore
- Fixed a bug with [[Bazaar]] crunching unstackable ([[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]]) items
- Fixed filtering [[Pets]] by [[Rarity]] on [[Auction House]] (newly listed pets only)
- Can now sell [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] above 5x min price
- [[Warren]] is going on vacation for a long long time
Good books now have a better rarity
Enjoy. :hypixel_wide_smile:

# Changelog/2020/May 21
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Protector QOL + Pet Balance + More
Updated: May 21, 2020
Hello,
Today I bring you quality of life changes for the [[End Stone Protector|Endstone Protector]] boss, pet balancing and bug fixes, and some general fixes/QOL changes.
Protector Changes:
- Drop chances are now influenced by "[[Zealot|Zealots]] Contributed" which caps at 100 representing how many zealot kills you contributed to spawning the boss
  - A high number of zealots contributed is required to get rare drops
  - You can see how many zealots you contributed via the summary after the boss has been defeated
  - Developer Note: this feature was added to provide a more fair experience and help prevent lobby-hopping in order to kill the boss without helping spawn it
- Added more spawn points
- Added more messaging/sounds
- Added [[Crystal Fragment|Crystal Fragments]] to the drop table for top 5 damagers
- Changed poppies to [[End Stone Rose|Endstone Roses]]
  - These roses can be collected or sold to a shop
Pet Changes:
- [[Snowman Pet|Snowman]]
  - Replaced all perks
  - Changed mob to a flying head
- [[Golem Pet|Golem]]
  - Fixed Toss damage calculating incorrectly and being too high
- [[Skeleton Pet|Skeleton]]
  - Changed arrow damage to 30x your crit damage
- [[Guardian Pet|Guardian]]
  - Stopped Lazer Beam from casting on players and killing them
- [[Black Cat Pet|Black Cat]]
  - Increased Pet Luck to 15
  - Clarified descriptions
- [[Phoenix Pet|Phoenix]]
  - Increased Fourth Flare damage to 15x crit damage
  - Fixed fourth flare sometimes not proccing
- [[Endermite Pet|Endermite]]
  - Nerfed Pearl Muncher coins to 10
  - Allow player dropped pearls to be munched
- [[Zombie Pet|Zombie]]
  - Increased Chomp hp gained to 25
- [[Giraffe Pet|Giraffe]]
  - Clarified description for Higher Ground
- [[Baby Yeti Pet|Yeti]]
  - Increased Yeti Fury intelligence buff to 100
- [[Dolphin Pet|Dolphin]]
  - Decreased Pod Tactics per-player speed to 5 but increased max players to 5
  - Increased Pod Tactics radius to 10 blocks and specified this in the perk description
- [[Pig Pet|Pig]]
  - Directly add the drops from Trample into the player inventory
- [[Flying Fish Pet|Flying Fish]]
  - Buffed Quick Reel fishing speed to 15%
- [[Ocelot Pet|Ocelot]]
  - Fixed Tree Essence not giving xp
- [[Blaze Pet|Blaze]]
  - Fixed Bling Armor not buffing the armor set ability
Quality of Life Changes:
- Removed potion limit
  - Developer Note: potion limits will play a part in dungeons and may play a part in other areas of the SkyBlock world in the future
- Removed monster kill [[Combat]] XP on private islands
  - Developer Note: having private island mobs giving combat XP has promoted too much abuse and was not the way the combat skill was intended to be trained
- Reintroduced [[Speed Ring]] and [[Speed Artifact]]
- Added double-tap to drop on pets and pet items
- Added item exclusivity for pet item drops
- Added two more pet score rewards
- Doubled the XP from all pet candy
- Fixed blindness and knockback counting as positive effects
- Made arrows picked up go directly into quiver
- Added a toggle to stop picking up arrows when quiver is full
- Added more details to the creative mind item
Bug Fixes:
- Fixed exp share applying bonus too hard
- Fixed [[Piggy Bank]] stacking
- Fixed [[Speed Talisman]] stacking
- Fixed [[Sea Creature Ring]]/artifact causing you to take no damage from sea creatures

# Changelog/2020/May 5
Aliases: 0.7.8
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.7.8
Taming Skill, Pet Items, Upgrading and More
Updated: May 5, 2020
## ▶ Taming Skill and Pet Score
A new skill has been added to SkyBlock as a companion to the pet's system. Taming XP is granted simply by leveling up pets. Leveling up the skill grants you extra pet XP and pet luck, a new stat that increases the chance to find pets in the wild and also increases the chance to craft better pets.
Along with the new taming skill, rewards have been added to collecting unique pets! Each unique pet now gives a score based on its rarity and upon hitting milestones of points you gain permanent magic find! Check it out by hovering over the diamond in the pets menu.
## ▶ New Pets
This update contains 16 new pets from various areas, we don't want to spoil too much but here are some ways you may find some of the new pets!
- Crafting
- Spooky Event Shop
- Zoo Event Shop
- Mobs around Slayer areas
- Fishing bosses
- Dark Auction
- Mining Ore
## ▶ Pet Items and Candy
Pet Items A new type of item has been added, Pet items! Pets can now hold specific items that give them special abilities or boosts but beware, pets can only hold one item at a time and they're consumed on use!
You can find pet items all across SkyBlock in shops, from killing mobs, and from crafting.
Pet Candy Is your pet craving a sweet treat? Craft some Pet Candy in the carrot collection and feet it your pet to give it an instant XP boost! Just be sure not to overfeed your pet, since they can only fit 10 candies in their bellies. Pet Candy comes in three tiers to cater to whatever financial situation you're in but beware, once your pet has eaten 10 candy, they can't eat anymore so choose wisely.
## ▶ New NPCs
A few new NPCs have arrived in town and settled in the Pets Building just west of spawn!
Kat the Pet Sitter
- Pay Kat to take care of your pet for a while and come back later to collect it once its rarity has been upgraded
Zog the Pet Item Vendor
- Buy various pet items to boost your pets!
George the Pet Collector
- Sell pets that need a new home to George for a pretty penny!
## ▶ New Mini-Boss
There have recently been reports of a malicious Endstone Protector lurking within the End. If any adventurers could defeat him they may just find themselves with a special new pet or pet item.
## ▶ Balancing and Small Changes
Balancing
- Giraffe
  - Changed Long Neck to become a combat perk
  - Buffed Higher Ground values
- Squid
  - Changed More Squids to something more useful
  - Swapped order of perks
- Silverfish
  - Buffed Dexterity to always give haste III
- Pigman
  - Replaced PVP King with Bacon Farmer
  - Buffed strength on Pork Master slightly
- Ocelot
  - Replaced Evasion with Tree Essence
- Wither Skeleton
  - Replaced Coal Crazy with Death's Touch
- Horse + Skeleton Horse
  - Added Ride into Battle perk
Small Changes
- Increased mining and fishing pet XP by 50%
- Fixed pagination of Pets Menu
- Increases max owned pets to 100
- Added magic find, sea creature chance, and pet luck to player profile
- Added active pet to pet menu lore
- Added active pet to player viewer (on right-click)
- Made fishing rods only deal damage to fishing mobs (to prevent AFK farming exploit)
- Small buffs to Blaze armor strength and defense
Note on future updates While we already have fun ideas for future Pets and Pet updates, the next Pet update won't be in development for a while. We will keep you updated on future Pet projects when the time comes! Concerning Dungeons, keep an eye on the forums this week, for a thread containing details on Alpha testing.
@sfarnham, @Jayavarmen - Design and Balancing @Minikloon, @erosemberg - Help with the Miniboss and Release @Bloozing, HPC - Testing/QA

# Changelog/2020/November 12
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.12
Guesting Update
Updated: November 12, 2020
Hi everyone,
Today we are releasing a new SkyBlock update, specifically a [[Guesting]] update! There are some new features that will make it easier to create and explore popular islands.
## Social Skill
A new skill has been added to the game! This skill is tied to your [[Profile]] and shared between [[Co-op]] members. You can gain [[Social]] experience through having unique [[Guests Management|guests]] visit [[Private Island|your island]], guests spending time on your island, and by visiting other people's islands yourself.
## Rewards
There are several rewards that are unlocked as you level up this skill. There are currently 10 rewards, with the potential for more in the future. A few examples of features you can unlock...
One reward allows you to [[Ranks|create ranks]] for your island (similar to Housing) with a custom name and color that you can give to [[Guests Management|guests]].
These tags give a nice chat prefix too! Each player can have one rank at a time.
Another reward is the ability to create [[Parkour Checkpoint|parkour courses]] on your island. Players can take part in them, passing through [[Parkour Checkpoint|checkpoints]], and receiving their time, like lobby parkour! You may create multiple parkour courses. There is also a purchasable item to display a hologram with guests' top times.
You can now unlock the ability to create [[Island NPC]]s! Create unique lore around your island - add dialogue to your NPCs to guide players around.
For many [[NPC]] mob types, you are able to edit individual settings such as a villager's profession as shown above or a player's skin.
A final reward example is egg hunt! This is similar to finding [[Fairy Souls]] but for your island. Guests can find them and gain points!
These are only a few of the rewards - level up and check out the others!
## Amelia's Shop
Although some of the skill's rewards are unlocked by default, many must be purchased from a new shop NPC in the hub - [[Amelia]]!
Find her near the [[Mountain]] on the way to the hot air balloon.
## Island Browser
There is a new menu accessed through [[Christopher]] in the hub, who you can find standing in the hot air balloon past [[Amelia]]. You are now able to browse the most [[Locations|popular islands]] in SkyBlock, sorting by various categories such as medieval build and dropper games.
Note: only islands that are able to be visited by all players are displayed in the island browser.
## Popularity Level
Each island in the browser is sorted by popularity level. An island's popularity is calculated from a combination of unique visitor count and the amount of time a player stays on the island. This level resets weekly.
Although popularity resets, your [[Social|social skill]] doesn't - to a certain extent you can think of it as your all-time island popularity!
## ► Island Management
There are some new features available to manage how your island is viewed in the island browser, as well as creating island [[Ranks]].
At a high social skill level, you can set a custom name for your island! These can include color codes too. Please be aware that we will remove and punish inappropriate names.
You can also select which category tags you would like assigned to your island (with a maximum of 3 at a time). These will be shown in the island browser, and used by players to find islands in your same category.
Find the island management menu in your [[SkyBlock Menu|SkyBlock settings]].
As a final note, we will likely adjust experience gain rates for the [[Social]] skill - as well as the qualifications for popularity levels - over the next few weeks!
We hope you enjoy this new update and are very excited to see what great things you add to your islands! Make sure to post in the Island Showcase sub-forum once you've created something cool.

# Changelog/2020/November 17
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.13
Catacombs Floor 7
Updated: November 17, 2020
Hello,
Over a year ago, we started working on the first SkyBlock [[Dungeons|Dungeon]]. Today, we are presenting you with the closing chapter of the [[Catacombs]], [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]]. We have also decided to raise the quality of the [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]] boss fight, but more details on that later though.
[[Necron]] is the master of the Catacombs and right hand of the [[Bosses|Wither King]]. Beating him will be extremely hard, but great rewards are at stake!
## Plans changed
Now that we're this far into [[Dungeons]], we have a good idea of how long it takes to create a floor. When we originally finished the [[Dungeons]] backend systems, we thought adding more floors would be a walk in the park - turns out it's not. The deeper we go into catacombs, the more complicated it gets.
Another thing to mention is that we'd planned to take a dungeons break at [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|floor 7]] all along. What actually changes, is that instead of releasing more [[Catacombs]] when we go back to dungeons, we can start working on a different kind of dungeon. Rest assured that the final challenge of the next dungeon will be harder than [[Necron]].
As such, we decided to change the [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]] fight to [[Necron|Necron's]]. While we still plan on releasing Master mode, players will have to wait at least a few months before we are ready to work on [[Dungeons]] again.
Gameplay changes:
- Players won't need to beat the whole [[Bosses|boss fight]] to claim rewards. Each phase of [[Necron|Necron's]] boss fight unlocks 1 extra chest, and beating [[Necron]] automatically unlocks the Obsidian chest even if the party didn't get an S.
- [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]] rewards a lot of Catacombs EXP, but depending on which phase you lost to, the EXP gain can be significantly lowered.
- The [[Spirit Bow]] now works with all classes, like it was intended to.
- The first 6 floors are now a little bit easier than before.
- Added a new [[Accessory Bag|talisman bag]] upgrade, from the [[Redstone Dust/Collection UI|redstone collection]].
- Buffed the [[Reaper Mask]] (health and defense)
That's it! We hope you will enjoy the boss fight, and we can't wait to show you our next projects!

# Changelog/2020/November 6
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.11
City Project: Farm Merchant's Dwelling
Updated: November 6, 2020
Hello!!
The community completed the third city project: Farm Merchant's Dwelling. Actually, it's renamed to "Farmhouse"!
## Farm Merchant's Dwelling Farmhouse
The [[Farmhouse]] offers a new event and many new [[Farming]] tools.
[[Jacob]] is a new NPC in the Farmhouse. He hosts the SkyBlock event: [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] every 3 SkyBlock days. The event lasts 1 SkyBlock day (20 irl minutes).
You need to be at least [[Farming]] level 10 to talk to [[Jacob]] AND participate in the events. Also, you need to talk to [[Jacob]] at least once to activate the event.
Every 3 days, you can compete in one of 3 [[Collections]]. You can know ahead which collection to farm by looking in the full [[Calendar]] menu. To participate in a contest, simply collect 100 of that item while the contest is running. Players who collect the most in one collection during the 20 minutes of the event earn rewards.
You will earn medals and [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] from participating in the event! Medals are profile-bound, you cannot trade them. Tickets are tradeable in the [[Bazaar]] under Oddities.
[[Anita]] is the other new NPC in the farmhouse. You can spend your medals and [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] in Anita's shop.
Anita offers items which are useful for building farms fast, and others for harvesting them fast. The items from Anita cannot be traded or placed in the [[Auction House]].
The discount from contributing to the [[City Projects|City Project]] reduces the Tickets cost of items. Also, medals can be converted and sold for Tickets.
## Double-drops Stacking
Because we added many items which impact it, we rebalanced the double-drops mechanic.
All mechanics which increase double-drops ( [[Farming]] Perk, [[Elephant Pet]], etc..) now stack additively. Essentially, when it says +50% chance for double-drops, it will add +50 to your hidden "double-drops" stat, which behaves like Ferocity. Double-drops above 100 turn into a chance for triple-drops (Just like [[Ferocity]]).
To compensate for this change, we doubled [[Elephant Pet]] perk to +60% and [[Monkey Pet]] to +50%.
## [[Auction House]] improvements
The AH will now remember your search settings when closed. Settings also reset after 20 minutes. There's a new button to reset the settings when you edited them.
Auctions search will now also display in the inventory window's title.
## General Changes
- Blessed [[Reforging|Reforge]] (from [[Smithmonger]]) is now available on Axes. Reforge from [[Blessed Fruit]].
- Added [[Cactus]] to the [[Farm Merchant]]
- Reshuffled the [[Bazaar]] (moved [[Slayer]] and [[Mythological Ritual]] to [[Combat]])
- Added contribution req. to Bartender's Brewery to let more people earn the perks. Contribution total raised from 500,000 to 1,000,000.
That's it! Enjoy farming!

# Changelog/2020/October 1
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.4
Catacombs Floor 6
Updated: October 1, 2020
Hello,
Today we are releasing the [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor 6]] of The Catacombs as well as many general changes! Sadan is a very powerful Necromancer with an army at his side!
A couple notes on some Floor 6 mechanics:
- The Boss being quite hard, the wooden and gold chests can be obtained without beating the whole boss fight. Your party still needs to reach certain points in the boss fight to unlock each chest.
- Some of the items rewarded in this Floor are a new take on the "mob summoning" mechanic. Instead of taking a % of the stats of the players, they now take the stats of the mobs captured and only aggro when the summoner is being hit. We also changed the [[Reaper Scythe]] to function the same way from now on. It's very likely that balancing will have to be made to that mechanic one way or another, but we believe this is a healthier version of that fun mechanic. Feedback needed!
Here is a list of a few general changes:
- Livids are now more likely to teleport to players if they are standing above ground.
- The "Drop Arrow" archer ghost ability is changed to a bow that allows you to heal and buff your alive teammates.
- Fixed an issue where the minimap would sometimes be cut on the sides.
- Reviving will now replenish your mana fully.
- Improved the UI of the Catacombs Menu and the icon of the Dungeoneering Skill.
- "Click to show rankings" in Skill Menu can now be toggled On/Off.
- Fixed "null" prefix on some mob tags.
- Fixed Dungeon reward chests only taking from personal banks.
- Fixed a couple edge cases that would cause players to lose their inventory on respawn, and added debugs and alerts to monitor potential new edge cases.
- Ghosts are now able to use abilities while in Danger rooms.
- All standing arrows are now being removed when [[End Stone Protector|Endstone Protectors]] spawn.
That's it! We are making good progress on Floor 7, after which we'll be done with floors for now to give players time to beat them, but also to allow us to spend more time on new Island content.
Also welcome @xHascox to the Admin team! He is a new Game Designer (like sfarnham and I) and will be working mostly on SkyBlock.

# Changelog/2020/October 2
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.5
Player List, Item Frames, and Bug Fixes!
Updated: October 2, 2020
Hello all,
Here is an exciting new set of features and bug fixes for you all today! Make sure to click to read more!
## Player List
For a long time now, SkyBlock has used the standard size (TAB) player list provided by Minecraft, but from now on, we've repurposed this list to help display even more useful information to you!
Additions:
- Condensed "Players" columns with quick access to how many players are currently online. (This is swapped out for COOP/Party members when on your island or in Dungeons respectively)
- COOP members, friends and Guild members are all shown with different colored stars next to their names!
- A "Server Info" column which will contain information specific to the server you are on (for example in The End it will show the current Dragon Fight and the top 3 players in terms of damage dealt).
- The "Global Info" column will be available everywhere on SkyBlock regardless of server and shows important information like your profile, bank balance, skills, upcoming events, and more!
- Improved "Active Effects", the potions effects section of the list will now display a condensed version of your potions showing only the soonest to expire.
- The new "/effects" command which acts as a replacement for the potion effects in the player list will sort all your potions by expiration!
- Varying player lists across different servers! For example, Dungeons has a different style of list than the Village or The Park.
We'll be continuously making adjustments and additions to the player list as we add new content so expect some awesome new features soon!
If you don't like the feature or your mod relies on the vanilla player list you can disable it in your SkyBlock settings too!
## Effects Menu
Of course, there is also the new effects menu similar to the menu added for disabling effects. However, this menu is always accessible by commands (/effects), or via the SkyBlock Menu, and will display all your active effects in order of soonest to expire!
## Item Frames
We know that vanilla item frames can be awkward at best, items can sometimes just disappear into ether never to be seen again! So, in a bid to keep the vanilla functionality but also save all your precious items I'm happy to introduce the new GUI for adding, viewing and removing items from item frames!
Sneak+Right-Click to open!
A cool extra that accompanies this change is that if a player is a guest visiting your island, they can open this menu but only view the item inside, not remove it!
Thus, this is now a fancy new display case Also, yes, my [[Farming Talisman]] is that precious to me
## Bug Fixes
- Ah everyone's favorite thing! I've got even more exciting bug fixes, many of them I know people have been asking to be fixed for a long time!
- Fixed the issues surrounding [[Toxic Arrow Poison]] and its drop protection
- Fixed item abilities using Wither Skulls from deleting dropped items, namely:
  - [[Soulstealer Bow|Crypt Soulstealer Bow]]
  - [[Dreadlord Sword|Crypt Dreadlord Sword]]
- Fixed the mana cost of [[Spider Shortbow|Scorpion Bows]]
- Allowed [[Ultimate Wise]] to work with [[Spider Shortbow|Scorpion Bows]]
- Fixed the Slayer Sack opening in the Slayer rewards menu
- For both Zombie & Spider rewards
- Fixed the /dungeoneering command opening the wrong menu
- Fixed being able to get free reforges
- Fixed the [[Skeleton Master Armor]] bonus not applying
- Made the [[Super Undead Bow]] actually work
- Fixed the [[Cleave]] enchantment dealing damage to island mobs when you were a guest
- Changed the [[Raider Sword|Raider Axe]] description to better fit its functionality
- Adds new menu UI to item frames (Shift+Right-Click) to safely add and remove items
- Also acts as a "display case" to island guests
- Fixed the [[Angler Armor]] giving invincibility against Sea Creatures
- Fixed not being able to clear auctions from deleted profiles
- Fixed being able to cancel auctions from another profile and claim the items from it
- Fixed being able to break the ladder on the Fisherman's Hut in the Hub
- Fixed a dupe bug with [[Training Weights]]
- Fixed [[Repelling Candle|Repelling Candles]] breaking into torches when placed on some blocks
- Fixed [[Repelling Candle|Repelling Candles]] sometimes replacing non solid blocks when placed
- Fixed [[Replenish]] not fetching items from your Sacks
- Fixed nicknames not showing up properly in certain profile GUIs
- Fixed being able to place enchanted:
  - Lava Buckets
  - Wool
  - Seeds
  - Poppys
  - Dandelions
  - Packed Ice

# Changelog/2020/October 20
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.8
City Project: Blacksmith Workspace + New Bits Shop items
Updated: October 20, 2020
Hello!
The community completed the second city project, the [[City Projects/Blacksmith Workspace|Blacksmith Workspace]].
Along with this update, we are releasing a few more features...
## Blacksmith Workspace
Upgraded Blacksmith area, now its own zone with two floors On top of its upgraded looks, the Blacksmith now houses the [[Smithmonger]] NPC.
The Smithmonger is a shop offering 5 [[Reforge Stones]]:
- Moil, 50k axe reforge, req Mining lvl 5, grants up to +3% foraging exp,
- Dirty, 100k sword/rods reforge, req Mining lvl 10, grants up to 15 STR, 20 AS & 15 Ferocity
- Toil, 1m axe reforge, req Mining lvl 25, grants up to 20 STR, 20 CD & +6% foraging exp
- Refined, 1m pickaxe reforge, req Mining lvl 25, grants up to 20 defense, +6% mining exp & +0.1% chance to drop the enchanted mined ores
- Blessed, 1m hoe reforge, req Mining lvl 25, grants up to 20 speed, 6% farming exp & +20% chance for double crops
The [[Smithmonger]] also offers a 2m [[Reforge Anvil]] for convenience, or you can use the anvil in front of him to apply the [[Reforge Stones|reforge stones.]] It is also believed half-price reforges have been removed.
## New Bits Shop Items
We added 3 new items to the [[Bits Shop]]: [[Builder's Wand|The Builder's Wand]] lets you build your dream farm (or anything) way faster
Functions like Expertise, but for Mining Accelerate your Bits acquisition (still capped by your Bits Available)
## New Stat: Ferocity
As seen in the player menu when you have at least 1 ferocity For the longest time, Attack Speed wasn't a functional stat in SkyBlock. During that period, one of our internal proposed solution was for AS to increase proc multiple strikes. We then added this behavior with a cool visual FX on the [[Tiger Pet]] and took a different path for Attack Speed.
But the idea is making a comeback in the form of a new damage stat!
Essentially, Merciless Swipe from [[Tiger Pet|Tiger]] is now a player stat which can appear on any item, pet, enchant, etc.. Updated [[Tiger Pet]], with +20 total Ferocity We retconned +5 Ferocity on [[Pigman Pet|Pigman]], [[Ghoul Pet|Ghoul]], [[Hound Pet|Hound]] & [[Megalodon Pet|Megalodon]] and +10 on [[Ocelot Pet]].
The Shredder also got a +50 Ferocity buff.
The new Dirty reforge has +15 Ferocity on [[Swords|Mythic swords]].
## Darker Auctions
As [[Scorpius]] is getting elected, there are a bundle of temporary new items in the [[Dark Auction]].
Since they are available for a limited time (5 days while [[Scorpius]] is enabled), we will share them here so that everyone has access to the info they need.
[[Scorpius]] auctions have 7 rounds, going as follow:
Item -> Book -> Item -> Book -> Scorpius Bonus -> [[Flower Minion]] -> [[Midas Staff|Midas' Staff]] Counter-Strike, applicable on your chestplate [[Enchanted Book Bundle|Book Bundles]] are a new concept to try and increase availability of these limited enchants a little bit. Big Brain is only applicable on your helmet Vicious is applicable to [[Swords]] The Vicious V book was also permanently added to the normal [[Dark Auction]] The [[Plasmaflux Power Orb|Plasmaflux]] actually sells in the DA as a recipe item It requires an [[Overflux Power Orb|Overflux]] in order to craft In case you wanted more stats Midas' Mage item
That's it for today!
Enjoy!

# Changelog/2020/October 30
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.9
Spooky Festival Update Halloween 2020
Updated: October 30, 2020
Hello there!
I hope everyone has their costumes ready because it's about to get spooky!
Yes, you heard right! It's that time of the year again where we all come together to decorate our homes, carve pumpkins, and go out trick-or-treating. But don't forget the most important part... play SkyBlock!
Today, we are adding a new update to the [[Spooky Festival]] containing new mobs, items, and rewards!
## Spooky Mobs
We added 6 new special spooky mobs which will only spawn during the Spooky Festival.
These mobs are:
- [[Wraith]]
- [[Crazy Witch|Batty Witch]]
- [[Trick or Treater]]
- [[Wither Gourd]]
- [[Phantom Spirit]]
- [[Scary Jerry]]
You will find these mobs in special areas on most islands, but they will only spawn during specific hours. These guys will be much more rewarding than normal mobs!
Dev Note
The idea here is to offer a similar time to candy ratio, regardless of the island you choose to participate in during the Spooky Festival. No matter what island you are on, you will be getting the same amount of candy from these mobs. Also, since these mobs only spawn during specific hours of in-game time, you will oftentimes find yourself rotating between mobs more often than before. This will hopefully add up to be a more interesting gameplay experience! These mobs are also fairly special, as they can only take 1 hp damage per hit with most weapons, so anyone can participate and perform equally, regardless of power level.
## Trick-or-Treat Loot
Killing mobs now during the Spooky Festival has a chance to drop a Trick-or-Treat Chest on the ground! These chests can have things like extra candy, new unique items, and some not so fortunate tricks...
Spooky Mobs will have a much higher chance to drop these chests.
## Mega Bat
Now and then, a Mega Bat will randomly appear on your server and will drop loads of candy per hit. So keep an eye out for this small scary-looking beast!
Maybe an item in the shop will help you locate this intimidating monstrosity.
## General Changes
- Added a [[Spooky Festival]] Point Counter to the tab
- Changed [[Bat Pet]] Fast Hooks ability to Wings of Steel, which now gives 50% extra damage towards spooky mobs
- Added a new tier of the Candy Accessory to the [[Fear Mongerer]]
- Fixed an issue with [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]], now they will be removed after death
Additionally, we are scheduling 5 extra Spooky Festivals for the next few days.
You can find out when these events occur for your timezone:
Happy Halloween!

# Changelog/2020/October 6
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.6
Ironman Gamemode
Updated: October 6, 2020
Hello,
Hypixel SkyBlock is releasing a new Special Mode: Ironman!
## What is Ironman?
Historically, Ironman is a term used to describe an MMORPG mode where players are unable to trade with other players, thus have to unlock every item and part of the game on their own. Some people prefer playing the game that way! You can now create a new Ironman Hypixel SkyBlock profile, solo or coop!
Changes between Ironman and regular profile:
- [[Auction House]] and [[Bazaar]] are disabled
- Trading is disabled between players (except your coop members)
- Picking up items from other players is disabled (except your coop members)
- Dropping items to other players is disabled (except your coop members)
- Cannot receive Winter island [[Gifts]] from other players
- Ironman players have an icon next to their name
## How to play
When creating a new profile, you now have the option to select a gamemode. Ironman is currently the only gamemode available.
You can simply left click to create a solo profile, or right click to create a coop with friends!
That's it, have fun! ♲

# Changelog/2020/September 17
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.1
Catacombs Floor 5 and general changes
Updated: September 17, 2020
Hello,
Today we are releasing the Floor 5 of The Catacombs, as long as many general changes! Good luck beating [[Livid]], he's a real trickster!
General Changes:
- Leaving a dungeon without killing the boss will now reduce the score by 30%
- The [[Lethality]] enchant has been fixed, it will now properly reduce the defense of enemy monsters
- Golden and Diamond Boss heads are not useless anymore. They grant some stats and if you wear them on the right floor, the stats are doubled!
- [[Dungeons]] stats are now added to the API
- Fixed a bug with the Mender helmets applying the heal buff to everyone
- The [[Combo]] enchant was buffed from 1 to 2 extra strength per stack
- Removed the damage cap on the [[Thorns]]
- Buffed [[Pigman Sword]] from granting 200 [[Defense]] to 300
- Added upgrades level 3 to 5 for a few Wither [[Essence Shops|Essence Shop]] perks
- Fix essence drops from dropping the incorrect amounts
- Fix being able to spend coins on the same dungeon chest twice if you have a dungeon key
- Added 4 new rooms and 3 room fixes
- Fixed [[Zombie Pet]] not buffing [[Zombie Knight Armor|Zombie Knight]]/[[Zombie Soldier Armor|soldier armor]], despite those being undeads
- Can use Thorn Boots ([[Spirit Boots]]) outside of dungeons now
- Floors 2, 3 and 4 are now approximately 5% easier
## Bedrock Chest
Starting Floor 5, obtaining an S+ rank will unlock a 6th chest, that can contain extremely strong items.
## Loot changes
We are experimenting with new loot systems:
Killing mobs in the [[Dungeons]] will now drop their loot client side. Whenever a mob drops loot, we now randomly distribute the item among eligible players. The killer, and all players within 20 blocks of the monster are all eligible for the roll. Nearby players have a weight of 1, and the killer has a weight of 5 to be selected.
Thanks for reading! Floor 6 and 7 are also right around the corner, we are going to give each floor release some breathing room before releasing them though!

Community Shop Patch
Updated: September 22, 2020
Hello!
Quick patch for a few community shop features!
## City Projects
You may now contribute multiple times to each [[City Projects|city project]] component. There is a daily cooldown between contributions on each component. We adjusted the material costs and fame rewards accordingly.
Players who previously contributed retain their contribution and the fame they earned. We didn't change the unique perk requirements this time, to keep it fair to past contributors.
## Bit Gains

- [[Ender Dragon|Dragons]]: 10 -> 15

That's it for now!

# Changelog/2020/September 23
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9.3
Co-op improvements
Updated: September 23, 2020
Hello,
Today we take the first step into making Co-ops great again! It's fun to share everything with your best friends, but waking up and seeing your bank account empty because your teammates decided to buy an overpriced [[Midas' Sword|Midas]]...can be a bad experience.
Here's the list of features:
## Personal bank accounts
Each member of the coop now have their own account, 20% the size of the current account upgrade. And each member can gain their own personal bank interests, on top of the shared account!
## Minion EXP
When a coop member collects minions, the Skill EXP now goes to each member. The EXP isn't shared, every member gets the full EXP even if they are offline when the minions gets collected.
## The Vault
This isn't just for Co-ops, but still an amazing feature for suspicious coop members! Tired of keeping your most valuable items in backpacks or in your Enderchest? Put them in your Vault, where they will be safe! Your Vault is also added to your profile menu, players can now check each other's Vaults. It's safe, but not secret!
And a few extra:
- If any members has /ignore'd someone, that person is now banned from the island. You don't need all members to /ignore anymore.
- Added /coopparty that sends a party invite to all your coop members
- Added /togglecoopchat or /co to toggle coop chat on so all your messages will be sent in coop chat
- Increased auction slots for co-ops by 3 for every co-op member above 1 (e.g. 4 in a co-op gets 9 extra slots)
Note: We are aware there was an issue on launch, we fixed it and reversed (most of) it. If you have negative coins in your purse don't worry! You got some extra coins and had to remove them from somewhere, you didn't actually lose any coins! If you encountered issues that have not been fixed yet, please open a bug report.
That's it for now! We hope you will enjoy those few features, we received many great suggestions and it was time to add some of the best ones. Thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2020/September 8
Categories: Changelogs missing descriptions, 2020 Updates, Updates
0.9
SkyBlock Patch 0.9 - Election Event & Community Shop
Updated: September 8, 2020
Hello!
Welcome to the [[Community Center]]!
Remember [[Clerk Seraphine|Mayor Clerk Seraphine]] and her friends [[Worker Emma]] & [[Worker Xavier]]? They finished their project! And it's big!
In a Nutshell:
- [[Mayor Election|Election Event]] every SkyBlock year
  - Includes 9 candidates, each with 3 perks
- 3 new special calendar events ([[Fishing Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta|National Mining Month]] & [[Mythological Ritual|Mythological Rituals]])
- New [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]] NPC to spend [[SkyBlock Gems]]
- Special Community Upgrades including:
  - Upgrade your private island size up to 2.1x
  - Increase your co-op size to 8 players
  - [[Minions|Minion]] Slots
  - The [[Booster Cookie]], the ultimate SkyBlock buff
  - [[City Projects]] for the community to work towards village upgrades
  - [[Fire Sale|FIRE Sales]] for exclusive cosmetics!
Keep reading to learn all about it!
## [[Mayor Election|Election Event]]
The [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] is a new calendar event which happens every SkyBlock year.
It's fairly simple! Each time it happens: 5 candidates are offered and you vote for your favorite!
There is a pool of 8 regular candidates + 3 special* candidates.
Regular Candidates:
- [[Candidate Barry|Barry]] the wizard
- [[Marina]] the fisherwoman
- [[Cole]] the miner
- [[Aatrox]], Maddox's brother-in-law
- [[Paul]] the dungeon explorer
- [[Diana]] the mythological pet carer
- [[Foxy]] the event girl
- [[Diaz]] the economist
- Special candidates only rarely choose to run for mayor.
Each candidate can offer up to 3 perks at once. All candidates start with 1 perk during their first election and have a chance to stack up to 3 perks if they are a returning candidate.
## [[Election Room|Election Booth​]]
The [[Election Room]] is open for about 4 real life days, then it closes for about 1 day. During the time the booth is open, you may change your vote. Once the election ends, the candidate with the most votes becomes Mayor for the next SkyBlock year and they grant their perk to every SkyBlock player for that time period.
## Election Perk Examples
### [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]]
The [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]] is a new, special shop run by [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]].
The shop offers many functionalities, divided into 6 tabs:
- [[City Projects]]
- Account & Profile Upgrades
- [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]]
- [[Bits Shop]]
- [[Fire Sale|FIRE Sales]]
- Hypixel Ranks
They all do different things, so let's go through all of them!
### [[City Projects]] allow the community to work and contribute towards upgrades to the village and beyond.
We're starting off with two projects: the Blacksmith Workspace and the Builder's House​
Each project has several components which you can contribute. Once enough contributions are accumulated throughout the community, the project goes into BUILDING mode, after which we'll shortly release the associated update to the village.
Apart from helping release the upgrade to the village, contributing grants you:
- A unique personal bonus
- A decent amount of Fame * We'll talk about Fame later. *
- City Project contributions count for all your profiles and you may only contribute once per component.
### Account & Profile Upgrades
Community Shop upgrades are a new set of improvements exclusive to the community shop. The upgrades are split into two categories:
- Profile upgrades, which apply to one profile and are shared with your co-op
- Account upgrades, which apply to all your profiles but only for you
Here are all the upgrades available on release:
#### Profile Upgrades
- Island Size: Expand your private island's size to 2.1x the default size​
- [[Minions|Minion]] Slots: Obtain up to +5 [[Minions|minion]] slots​
- Guests Limit: Increase your island's guest limit up to +5 guests​
- Co-op Slots: Invite up to +3 players to your co-op​
- [[Coins]] Allowance: Each co-op member gains a daily coin bonus of up to 50k [[Coins|coins​]]
#### Account Upgrades
- [[Ender Chest]] Pages: Use up to +8 ender chest pages (3 rows each)
- [[Accessory Bag|Talisman Bag]]: Unlock up to +6 talisman bag slots
- [[Sack of Sacks]]: Add +5 slots to your Sack of Sacks
- [[Magic Find]]: Gain up to +5 permanent Magic Find
- [[Bazaar]] Flipper: Run more concurrent Bazaar orders and reduce Bazaar sell taxes
Unlocking a community upgrade is done in 2 steps.
You first start an upgrade, then there's a pre-defined upgrade time, and once it's ended you claim the upgrade to activate it. All the upgrades are tiered with different amounts of tiers for each type.
For example, the first [[Minions|Minion]] Slot upgrade lasts 1 (real life) day.
You can have two upgrades running at the same time: one Profile and one Account. Remember, profile upgrades are shared with your co-op, so anyone on a co-op profile may pick and start the profile upgrade.
Most of the community upgrades can be obtained completely for free. Some of the higher tiers of certain upgrades aren't free, for example the Island Size. The Co-op Slots upgrade is the only one that costs something to unlock tier 1.
All upgrade tiers making your profile/character more powerful are available for free.
Upgrades and tiers which aren't free cost [[SkyBlock Gems]]. [[SkyBlock Gems]] are a new currency that you may purchase on the Hypixel Store.
You are also able to skip the upgrade time using [[SkyBlock Gems]]. Skipping the time of a profile upgrade on a co-op will bypass the time for every member.
### [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]]
The [[Booster Cookie]] is a new consumable item sold in the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]]. It is purchasable for [[SkyBlock Gems]] in the [[Elizabeth|community shop]], but may also be purchased and exchanged by players for [[Coins]] in the [[Bazaar]].
The Cookie Buff lasts 4 days per cookie. You may eat as many cookies as you want to add to your buff's duration. [[Elizabeth]] offers one free (untradable) cookie to every player, which you can use to try out the buff.
Once you eat your first cookie, you will unlock the Booster Cookie Menu in your [[SkyBlock Menu]].
### Bits Shop
While the Cookie Buff is active, you gain the ability to earn and find Bits in SkyBlock.
They're not given directly: they are awarded while your cookie buff is active!
For example, you will earn bits over time by simply playing the game, but you may also earn them from completing dungeons, killing dragons, and various other tasks. Each time you consume a [[Booster Cookie]], you gain the ability to find an extra 4,800 Bits.
Once earn, bits allow you to purchase items in [[Elizabeth|Elizabeth's]] [[Bits Shop]]:
### [[Fire Sale|FIRE Sales]]
[[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] is a new mechanic for us to deliver exclusive cosmetic items.
They're simple: A countdown shows for the next [[Fire Sale]] in the shop Once it starts, there is a limited amount of items for sale After the item is sold out: That's it! The sale is over! The items may or may never come back on sale.
We implemented new types of cosmetics and stockpiled a ton of them for upcoming fire sales. There are now Pet Skins, Armor Skins, new [[Minion Skins]] and [[Runes]].
### Hypixel Ranks
Finally, since SkyBlock's release, we always had Hypixel rank features, but they've never been fully documented in one place. The rank benefits are now laid out plainly for all in the Community Shop and will be kept up to date:
Support SkyBlock's development, purchase [[SkyBlock Gems|Gems]], Ranks and unlock exclusive rewards: https://store.hypixel.net
Whew! That's it for this update! I hope to see you all in game, I'm excited to see which candidate will be elected mayor first!

# Abysmal Lasso
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Vinesap Collection, Tools
[Abysmal Lasso]
Type: Lasso
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Vinesap I (1) ([[Vinesap]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Vinerip Lasso]]

[Materials]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ABYSMAL_LASSO
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; This is barely holding together, seriously who made this?; Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK Throw the Lasso at Forest creatures and hold them still until they lose all their stamina.; Ability: Reel In RIGHT CLICK Reel in creatures caught by the Lasso each time their stamina depletes.; * Soulbound * COMMON LASSO
An Abysmal Lasso is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Lassos|Lasso]]. It can be received for free or crafted with [[Vinesap|Vinesap I (1)]] collection.
## Obtaining
### Auryon
An Abysmal Lasso is given by [[Auryon]] during [[David Hunterborough|David's Hunting Lessons]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap I (1) ([[Vinesap]])|5x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] ×5 → Abysmal Lasso|
## Usage
An Abysmal Lasso can be thrown at forest creatures to catch them. While held, a stamina bar appears above its head. When the bar reaches around 20%, a prompt to reel appears. Right-clicking will deplete the stamina bar and pull in the mob.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vinerip Lasso]]|Vinesap II (2) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] [[Abysmal Lasso]] 1x Abysmal Lasso 12x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x4 ×3 + Abysmal Lasso + [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] → [[Vinerip Lasso]]|

Item ID: LARGE_ENCHANTED_AGRONOMY_SACK
Tooltip: Holds various items obtained from Farming! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 640 of each item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open!; COMMON SACK
The Agronomy Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Wheat]] collection at [[Wheat|Wheat II (2)]], [[Wheat|Wheat V (5)]], [[Wheat|Wheat VIII (8)]], [[Wheat|Wheat X (10)]], and [[Wheat|Wheat XI (11)]] with increasing capacity. The Small Agronomy Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Agronomy Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Agronomy Sack can hold 20,160 of each item. Beginner Sack holds 640 items, but only includes vanilla resources. The Large Enchanted Agronomy Sack can hold 20,160 of enchanted items.
## Obtaining
### Merchant
A Beginner Agronomy Sack can be bought for 250 [[Coins]] at [[Farm Merchant]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] near the [[Farm]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|Wheat II (2) ([[Wheat]])|1x [[Leather]] 8x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Wheat]] ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Agronomy Sack]]|
|Small Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|Wheat V (5) ([[Wheat]])|1x Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Wheat]] ×8 + [[Agronomy Sack]] → [[Agronomy Sack]]|
|Medium Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|Wheat VIII (8) ([[Wheat]])|1x Small Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]) 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Wheat]] x32 ×8 + [[Agronomy Sack]] → [[Agronomy Sack]]|
|Large Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|Wheat X (10) ([[Wheat]])|1x Medium Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]) 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Enchanted Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Wheat]] x64 ×8 + [[Agronomy Sack]] → [[Agronomy Sack]]|
|Large Enchanted Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|Wheat XI (11) ([[Wheat]])|12x [[Enchanted Leather]] 16x [[Enchanted String]] 16x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x3 ×2 + [[Enchanted String]] x16 + [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x2 ×5 + [[Enchanted Leather]] x12 → [[Agronomy Sack]]|
## Usage
The Agronomy Sack can store [[Farming]]-related items.
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|640|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), [[Cactus]], [[Carrot]], [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Melon Slice]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Poisonous Potato]], [[Potato]], [[Pumpkin]], Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), [[Seeds]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Wheat]]|
|Small Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|2,240|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), [[Cactus]], [[Cactus Green]], [[Carrot]], [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Golden Carrot]], [[Hay Bale]], [[Jack o' Lantern]], [[Melon Slice]], [[Moonflower]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Poisonous Potato]], [[Potato]], [[Pumpkin]], Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), [[Seeds]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Sunflower]], [[Wheat]], [[Wild Rose]]|
|Medium Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|
|Large Enchanted Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])|20,160|[[Box of Seeds]], [[Compacted Moonflower]], [[Compacted Sunflower]], [[Compacted Wild Rose]], [[Enchanted Baked Potato]], [[Enchanted Bread]], [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]], [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]], [[Enchanted Cactus]], [[Enchanted Cactus Green]], [[Enchanted Carrot]], [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]], [[Enchanted Cookie]], [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]], [[Enchanted Hay Bale]], [[Enchanted Melon]], [[Enchanted Melon Slice]], [[Enchanted Moonflower]], [[Enchanted Nether Wart]], [[Enchanted Poisonous Potato]], [[Enchanted Potato]], [[Enchanted Pumpkin]], [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]], [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]], [[Enchanted Seeds]], [[Enchanted Sugar]], [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]], [[Enchanted Sunflower]], [[Enchanted Wheat]], [[Enchanted Wild Rose]], [[Mutant Nether Wart]], [[Polished Pumpkin]]|
## Bugs
- [[Cropie]], [[Squash]] and [[Compost]] stored inside the [[Garden Sack]] will incorrectly appear in the Agronomy Sack, despite the tooltip displaying that "This item has been removed from this sack! After withdrawal it can no longer be deposited into this sack."
## Trivia
- [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]] will not automatically compact items inside the sack until the items are out of the sack.

Item ID: PET_ITEM_ALL_SKILLS_BOOST_COMMON
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +10% pet exp for all skills.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
All Skills Exp Boost is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It grants +10% [[Pets|Pet]] Exp for all [[Skills]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
All Skills Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a All Skills Exp Boost will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +10% [[Pets|Pet]] Exp for all [[Skills]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the All Skills Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting

|[[All Skills Exp Super-Boost]]|None|1x [[Zog Anvil]] [[All Skills Exp Boost]] 1x All Skills Exp Boost 18x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x6 ×3 + All Skills Exp Boost + [[Zog Anvil]] → [[All Skills Exp Super-Boost]]|

Angler Armor is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Armor]] set that is purchased from the [[Fishing Merchant]]. It is designed to be an introductory [[Fishing]]-based armor set.
## Obtaining
### Fishing Merchant
Each piece of Angler Armor and [[Angler Armor|Angler Equipment]] can be purchased from the [[Fishing Merchant]] for 100 [[Coins]].
## Usage

|[[Angler Necklace]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +1 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +0.5|[[Angler Cloak]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +1 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +0.5|
|Belt|Bracelet|
|[[Angler Belt]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +1 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +0.5|[[Angler Bracelet]]; [[Fishing Speed]]: +1 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +0.5|
[Total]
[Angler Armor Defense: +105 Fishing Speed: +4 Sea Creature Chance: +4]
[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Depth Champion; Reduces the Damage taken from Sea Creatures by 3-10%. Tiered Bonus: Deepness Within; Gain 6-10 Health per Fishing Level.]
Angler Armor's Depth Champion tiered bonus reduces damage taken from [[Sea Creatures]] by 3-10%, and its Deepness Within tiered bonus grants +6-10 [[Health]] per [[Fishing]] level.

- Angler Armor, due to its low price and damage reduction from Sea Creatures, should be your first set of Fishing armor.

The Ant Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Queen Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Ant Shard can be obtained from killing an [[Ant]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
An Ant Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Ant's Cave]].
### Fusion
An Ant Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Ant Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Queen Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Queen Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] towards Queen Ants ([[Queen Ant]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Ant Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Ant Shard has the following description:
- Ants are incredibly strong, being able to carry around 20x their own body weight.

The Archaeologist's Compass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
The Archaeologist's Compass is obtained by talking to the [[Archaeologist]].
The Archaeologist's Compass can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] in the [[Gold Mine]] for 20,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking with the Archaeologist's Compass will point in the direction of the nearest [[Relics|Relic]] on the [[Spider's Den]]. It only works when on the [[Spider's Den]].
## Trivia
- If the [[Player]] has already started the [[Relics]] [[Quests|Quest]], they will have to purchase it from [[Rusty]], as the [[Archaeologist]] only grants the item at the beginning of the [[Quests|Quest]].
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Personal Compactor 4000|Personal Compactor]], [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], Archaeologist's Compass, [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.

# Ashwreath
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Ashwreath]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASHWREATH
Tooltip: It rises from ashen ground, gray and whispering.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
An Ashwreath is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
An Ashwreath can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Nether Wart]] and 2x [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Fire]] within 1 block of [[Soul Sand]].
When placed near another crop, Ashwreath increases yield by 30% and reduces [[Farming]] experience by -20%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Ashwreath also drops 180 (2 stacks plus 52) [[Nether Wart]] and +240 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
An Ashwreath can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing an Ashwreath grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Ashwreaths are used to create a [[Cindershade]], [[Coalroot]], and [[Blastberry]].
## Gallery
- Ashwreath in the Greenhouse Ashwreath in the [[Greenhouse]]

# Aspect of the Jerry
Aliases: AOTJ, AotJ, Aotj, Aspect Of The Jerry, Aspect of Jerry, Aspect of jerry, Thick Aspect of the Jerry
Categories: Common items, Weapons, Craftable items, Swords, Items, Melee Weapons, Wood
[Aspect of the Jerry]
Also known as: AOTJ
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: 1
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2x [[Move Jerry]] 1x [[Stick]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASPECT_OF_THE_JERRY
Tooltip: Damage: +1; Ability: Parley RIGHT CLICK Channel your inner Jerry.; Cooldown: 5s; This item can be reforged!; COMMON SWORD
Aspect of the Jerry is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Swords|Sword]]. The Aspect of the Jerry started as a joke created by 54[[Ranks|[INNIT]]] TommyInnit, which the developers then added into the game.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Stick]] 2x [[Move Jerry]]|Recipe: [[Move Jerry]] ×2 + [[Stick]] → Aspect of the Jerry|
## Usage
Sword:
Aspect of the Jerry [[Damage]]: +1
[Effects]
[Ability: Parley RIGHT CLICK; Channel your inner Jerry.; Cooldown: 5s]
## Trivia
- The Ultimate Enchantment [[Ultimate Jerry|Ultimate Jerry V]] says it increases the base damage of the Aspect of the Jerry by 5000%, but actually only increases it by 4,900%, because it multiplies it by 5000%
- For a short time the AOTJ became one of the best weapons in the game. There is a reforge called the [[Jerry Stone|Jerry reforge]] giving the AOTJ the ability to convert all intelligence to damage. The devs however, made it so that the damage was added before the multipliers so people could enter a dungeon with [[Storm's Armor]], use the ability, then switch to a Tux Set dealing hundreds of millions of damage. This bug was patched on the same day the Jerry Reforge was released.
  - The YouTuber Palikka was able to do over a billion damage with the bugged Jerry reforge.
- Recombobulating an Aspect of the Jerry will give the player the [[Achievements|Achievement]] [[Achievements|Seriously?]]
- Normally, the Aspect of the Jerry is the worst sword in the game for base stats AND the ability since its damage is close to nothing. One should not use this sword for actual combat, because every other weapon with the same upgrades outclasses this, even a vanilla wooden sword. A player should only max this out if the player has spare money and doesn't know how to spend it.
## Gallery
Maxed Aspect of The Jerry
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

# Auto Smelter
Aliases: Autosmelter
Categories: Common items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Cobblestone Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Auto Smelter]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Cobblestone III (3) ([[Cobblestone]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 32 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[Cobblestone]] 1x [[Coal]]
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 192 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AUTO_SMELTER
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. This will automatically smelt materials that a minion produces.; COMMON
Auto Smelter is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Minions|Minion upgrade]] which automatically smelts everything the minion produces, which can be very useful for Ore [[Minions]], like [[Iron Minion]] or [[Gold Minion]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone III (3) ([[Cobblestone]])|1x [[Coal]] 64x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] x8 ×8 + [[Coal]] → Auto Smelter|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Auto Smelter can be placed in the minions listed below. It automatically smelts the materials harvested by the minion. This upgrade is useful for [[Minions]] such as the [[Iron Minion]], which require the smelted version to upgrade. It can only be placed in the Upgrade slot.
|Minion|Item|Smelted Version|
|-|-|-|
|[[Iron Minion]]|Iron Ore ([[Ores]])|[[Iron Ingot]]|
|[[Gold Minion]]|Gold Ore ([[Ores]])|[[Gold Ingot]]|
|[[Cactus Minion]]|[[Cactus]]|[[Cactus Green]]|
|[[Sand Minion]]|[[Sand]]|[[Glass]]|
|[[Oak Minion]]|[[Oak Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Spruce Minion]]|[[Spruce Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Birch Minion]]|[[Birch Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Dark Oak Minion]]|[[Dark Oak Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Acacia Minion]]|[[Acacia Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Jungle Minion]]|[[Jungle Log]]|[[Coal]]|
|[[Cobblestone Minion]]|[[Cobblestone]]|[[Stone]]|
|[[Clay Minion]]|[[Clay Ball]]|[[Bricks]]|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|Mithril VI (6) ([[Mithril]])|1x [[Super Compactor 3000]] [[Auto Smelter]] 1x Auto Smelter 56x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x8 ×7 + Auto Smelter + [[Super Compactor 3000]] → [[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Auto Smelter.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Auto Smelter now works with [[Cactus]] to produce [[Cactus Green]].|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Auto Smelter to the [[Bazaar]].|

# Azure Shard
Categories: Common items, Shards
[Azure Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: C29
Families: Tropical Fish ([[Shard Families]])
Skill: Combat
Category: Water
[Attribute]
Name: Forest Strength ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Grants +5–50 [[Strength]] while on Foraging Islands.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: AZURE
Tooltip: Forest Strength I Grants +5  Strength while on Foraging Islands.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON WATER SHARD (ID C29)
The Azure Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Forest Strength]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Azure Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] an [[Azure]].
### Huntraps
An Azure Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
An Azure Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Azure Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Forest Strength]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Forest Strength ([[Attributes]]); Grants +5–50 [[Strength]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Azure Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Azure Shard has the following description:
- Known for their agile movements, they dart between submerged roots and plants with ease.

The Beastmaster Crest is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] available during [[Diana|Diana's]] [[Mythological Ritual]]. It is available in 5 rarities and the pet XP bonus perks scale depending on how many [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]] the player has slain. The Kill Count is tied to the Profile, not the accessory. A minimum Kill Count of 10,000 is required to max out the [[Accessories|accessory]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

## Usage
Beastmaster Crest grants bonus [[Pets|Pet]] XP, [[Intelligence]] and [[Health]], depending on the amount of [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]] the player has killed.

## Trivia
- All Pet XP boosts work multiplicatively together, [[Taming]], [[Diana]] and [[Pet Items]].
